| 722 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1957 | Herbert Simon: Models of Man Social and Rational Essays | Herbert A. Simon, Carnegie Tech | Formalizes mathematical models of human choice, linking social influence networks with bounded cognitive rationality. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 723 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1957 | Leon Festinger: A Theory of Cognitive Dissonance Published | Leon Festinger, Stanford Univ | Holding contradictory beliefs creates intolerable psychic tension requiring cognitive resolution. | `social_psych` |
| 724 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1957 | Melanie Klein: Envy and Gratitude Object Relations Text | Melanie Klein, British Psychoanalytical | Distinguishes destructive primal envy toward the caregiving breast from mature reparative gratitude in infancy. | `psychoanalysis` |
| 725 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1957 | Noam Chomsky: Syntactic Structures Challenges Behaviorism | Noam Chomsky, MIT | Demonstrates universal generative grammar cannot be acquired via Skinnerian conditioning, igniting cognitive revolution. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 726 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1957 | Osgood: Measurement of Meaning (Semantic Differential) | Charles E. Osgood, Univ of Illinois | Measures affective connotative meaning of concepts along three dimensions: Evaluation, Potency, and Activity. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 727 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1957 | Sears, Maccoby & Levin: Patterns of Child Rearing Study | Robert Sears, Eleanor Maccoby & Harry Levin | Interviews 379 American mothers on feeding, toilet training, and conscience development via warmth vs punishment. | `developmental_psych` |
| 728 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Donald Broadbent: Perception and Communication Published | Donald E. Broadbent, Cambridge MRC | Seminal information-processing framework modeling the brain as a multi-channel telecommunication receiver. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 729 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Donald Super: Vocational Development Career Self-Concept | Donald E. Super, Columbia Teachers College | Frames career choice as lifelong developmental implementation of an individuals evolving self-concept. | `industrial_org` |
| 730 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Evelyn Hooker: Adjustment of Overt Male Homosexuals | Evelyn Hooker, UCLA | Blind Rorschach and TAT evaluations prove homosexual and heterosexual men display identical psychological adjustment. | `clinical_psych` |
| 731 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Frank Rosenblatt: The Perceptron Single-Layer Neural Network | Frank Rosenblatt, Cornell Aero Lab | Early artificial neural network computer capable of learning visual pattern recognition via weighted synaptic updates. | `ai_psych` |
| 732 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Fritz Heider: The Psychology of Interpersonal Relations | Fritz Heider, Univ of Kansas | Foundational attribution theory: introduces internal vs external attributions and cognitive balance theory. | `social_psych` |
| 733 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Harry Harlow: The Nature of Love (Cloth vs Wire Mothers) | Harry Harlow, Univ of Wisconsin | Infant rhesus monkeys cling to soft terrycloth surrogate mother rather than bare wire mother providing milk. | `developmental_psych` |
| 734 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Joseph Wolpe: Psychotherapy by Reciprocal Inhibition | Joseph Wolpe, Stanford & Wits | Formalizes systematic desensitization for anxiety disorders, pairing deep muscle relaxation with phobia hierarchies. | `clinical_psych` |
| 735 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Lawrence Kohlberg: Six Stages of Moral Development Thesis | Lawrence Kohlberg, Univ of Chicago | Uses the Heinz drug-stealing dilemma to map pre-conventional, conventional, and post-conventional moral reasoning. | `developmental_psych` |
| 736 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1958 | Rollo May: Existence: New Dimension in Psychiatry & Psych | Rollo May, Ernest Angel & Ellenberger | Introduces European phenomenological and existential psychotherapy to American clinical psychology. | `clinical_psych` |
| 737 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Aronson & Mills: Severity of Initiation Dissonance Study | Elliot Aronson & Judson Mills, Stanford | Women undergoing embarrassing initiation to join a dull discussion group rate the group as significantly more appealing. | `social_psych` |
| 738 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | David Hubel & Torsten Wiesel: Receptive Fields of Visual | David Hubel & Torsten Wiesel, Johns Hopkins | Discovers orientation-selective simple and complex feature detector neurons in primary visual cortex of cats. | `neuropsychology` |
| 739 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Eleanor Gibson & Richard Walk: The Visual Cliff Depth | Eleanor J. Gibson & Richard Walk, Cornell | Crawling 6-month-old human infants refuse to crawl onto transparent glass cliff, proving innate depth perception. | `developmental_psych` |
| 740 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Erving Goffman: The Presentation of Self in Everyday Life | Erving Goffman, Edinburgh & Penn | Dramaturgical sociological psychology: individuals perform on social front stages while hiding true motives backstage. | `social_psych` |
| 741 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Festinger & Carlsmith: Consequences of Forced Compliance | Leon Festinger & J. Carlsmith, Stanford | Subjects paid $1 to lie that a boring spool task was fun report genuinely enjoying it, unlike subjects paid $20. | `social_psych` |
| 742 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Frederick Herzberg: Two-Factor Hygiene-Motivator Theory | Frederick Herzberg, Case Western Reserve | Separates workplace job dissatisfaction hygiene factors (pay, policy) from intrinsic motivators (achievement, growth). | `industrial_org` |
| 743 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Milton Rokeach: The Three Christs of Ypsilanti Case Study | Milton Rokeach, Ypsilanti State Hospital | Brings three paranoid schizophrenic men claiming to be Jesus Christ together for two years to study delusional identity. | `clinical_psych` |
| 744 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1959 | Noam Chomsky: Review of B.F. Skinners Verbal Behavior | Noam Chomsky, Language Journal | Critique exposing behaviorisms inadequacy in explaining linguistic productivity and child language acquisition. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 745 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | Anne Treisman: Attenuation Theory of Selective Attention | Anne Treisman, Oxford University | Modifies Broadbent filter model: unattended channels are attenuated rather than erased, allowing name breakthrough. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 746 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | Douglas McGregor: Human Side of Enterprise (Theory X & Y) | Douglas McGregor, MIT Sloan | Contrasts authoritarian workplace control (Theory X) with humanistic empowerment and self-direction (Theory Y). | `industrial_org` |
| 747 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | Endel Tulving: Subjective Organization in Free Recall | Endel Tulving, Univ of Toronto | Proves learners actively impose idiosyncratic semantic clustering on unrelated word lists across repeated recall trials. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 748 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | George Sperling: Iconic Memory Duration in Tachistoscope | George Sperling, Harvard Dissertation | Partial report paradigm proves iconic visual sensory memory holds vast visual scene for approximately 250 milliseconds. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 749 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | Jerome Bruner & George Miller: Center for Cognitive Studies | Jerome Bruner & George A. Miller | Founds dedicated interdisciplinary haven for experimental cognitive psychology, language, and memory research. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 750 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | Miller, Galanter & Pribram: Plans and Structure of Behavior | George Miller, Galanter & Pribram | Introduces the TOTE unit (Test-Operate-Test-Exit) cybernetic feedback loop replacing linear stimulus-response reflex. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 751 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | R.D. Laing: The Divided Self Existential Study of Madness | Ronald David Laing, Tavistock Clinic | Interprets psychosis not as brain disease but as an intelligible existential defense against ontological insecurity. | `clinical_psych` |
| 752 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1960 | Thomas Szasz: The Myth of Mental Illness Published | Thomas Szasz, Syracuse University | Provocative anti-psychiatry manifesto arguing psychiatric diagnoses are moral judgments stigmatizing non-conformity. | `clinical_psych` |
| 753 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | Albert Bandura: The Bobo Doll Social Learning Experiment | Albert Bandura, Dorothea Ross & Sheila Ross | Children observing adult beat inflatable Bobo doll aggressively imitate novel physical and verbal violence. | `social_psych` |
| 754 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | Association for Humanistic Psychology Founded | Maslow, Rogers, May & Allport | The Third Force in psychology: rejects behaviorist mechanism and psychoanalytic determinism for human potential. | `positive_psych` |
| 755 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | Carl Rogers: On Becoming a Person Humanistic Philosophy | Carl Rogers, Univ of Wisconsin | Bestselling manifesto describing the fully functioning person characterized by open experience and self-trust. | `positive_psych` |
| 756 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | Erving Goffman: Asylums and Total Institution Mortification | Erving Goffman, NIMH Bethesda | Ethnographic field study exposing how psychiatric hospitals strip patient identity and induce institutionalization. | `social_psych` |
| 757 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | J. McVicker Hunt: Intelligence and Experience Enrichment | J. McVicker Hunt, Univ of Illinois | Refutes fixed genetic IQ dogma, demonstrating early environmental enrichment plasticly elevates child cognitive growth. | `developmental_psych` |
| 758 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | Jerome Frank: Persuasion and Healing Common Therapy Factors | Jerome D. Frank, Johns Hopkins | Argues all effective psychotherapies share common healing mechanisms: therapeutic alliance, hope, and healing myth. | `clinical_psych` |
| 759 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1961 | Stanley Milgram: Obedience to Authority Shock Study at Yale | Stanley Milgram, Yale University | 65% of ordinary participants administer maximum 450-volt electric shocks to screaming victim when ordered by authority. | `social_psych` |
| 760 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Abraham Maslow: Toward a Psychology of Being | Abraham Maslow, Brandeis Univ | Examines peak experiences, Being-values (B-values), and the psychological defense of the Jonah Complex. | `positive_psych` |
| 761 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Albert Ellis: Reason and Emotion in Psychotherapy | Albert Ellis, New York City | Comprehensive handbook formalizing Rational Emotive Behavior Therapy for overcoming destructive cognitive dogmas. | `clinical_psych` |
| 762 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Brenda Milner: Mirror-Tracing Procedural Memory in H.M. | Brenda Milner, Montreal Neurological | Amnesic patient H.M. masters mirror-star tracing over three days without conscious recall, dissociating motor memory. | `neuropsychology` |
| 763 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Esalen Institute Founded in Big Sur California | Michael Murphy & Richard Price | Epicenter of the Human Potential Movement hosting Gestalt therapy, encounter groups, and holistic psychology. | `positive_psych` |
| 764 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Michael Gazzaniga & Roger Sperry: Split-Brain Patient W.J. | Michael Gazzaniga & Roger Sperry, Caltech | Callosotomy patient demonstrates independent visual and linguistic awareness in disconnected cerebral hemispheres. | `neuropsychology` |
| 765 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Sarnoff Mednick: Remote Associates Test (RAT) of Creativity | Sarnoff A. Mednick, Univ of Michigan | Measures creative insight as the ability to forge associative bridges between mutually distant semantic concepts. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 766 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Silvan Tomkins: Affect Imagery Consciousness Volume One | Silvan S. Tomkins, Princeton University | Identifies nine innate biological primary affects displayed on the face as the core motivational engine of human psyche. | `experimental_psych` |
| 767 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1962 | Stanley Schachter & Jerome Singer: Two-Factor Emotion Theory | Stanley Schachter & Jerome Singer, Columbia | Proves emotional experience requires both physiological autonomic arousal and cognitive environmental labeling. | `social_psych` |
| 768 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1963 | Aaron Beck: Thinking and Depression (Cognitive Triad Dawn) | Aaron T. Beck, Univ of Pennsylvania | Cognitive dissonance causes subjects to inflate value of group membership after undergoing severe initiation rituals. | `clinical_psych` |
| 769 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1963 | Albert Bandura: Social Learning and Personality Development | Albert Bandura & Richard Walters | Comprehensive framework of vicarious learning, observational modeling, and symbolic reinforcement. | `social_psych` |
| 770 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1963 | Nikolaas Tinbergen: On Aims and Methods of Ethology | Nikolaas Tinbergen, Oxford University | Establishes four complementary levels of behavioral explanation: proximate causation, ontogeny, function, and phylogeny. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 771 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1963 | Raymond Cattell: Fluid vs Crystallized Intelligence Theory | Raymond B. Cattell, Univ of Illinois | Splits general intelligence into biological problem-solving fluid ability (Gf) and acquired cultural knowledge (Gc). | `cognitive_psych` |
| 772 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1963 | Stanley Milgram: Behavioral Study of Obedience Published | Stanley Milgram, JASP | Publishes shocking experimental findings demonstrating the perilous vulnerability of civilized conscience to authority. | `social_psych` |
| 773 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1963 | Stanley Milgram: The Lost-Letter Technique for Attitudes | Stanley Milgram, Yale University | Drops stamped letters addressed to controversial organizations on city streets to unobtrusively measure community bias. | `social_psych` |
| 774 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1964 | Eric Berne: Games People Play (Transactional Analysis) | Eric Berne, San Francisco | Popular psychodynamic framework decoding interpersonal transactions between Parent, Adult, and Child ego states. | `social_psych` |
| 775 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1964 | Marian Diamond: Enriched Environments Grow Cerebral Cortex | Marian Diamond, Mark Rosenzweig & Krech | Rats raised in toy-filled social cages develop 6% thicker cerebral cortex and larger synapses than isolated rats. | `neuropsychology` |
| 776 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1964 | Rosenthal & Jacobson: Pygmalion in the Classroom Effect | Robert Rosenthal & Lenore Jacobson | Teachers false high expectations for randomly selected students become self-fulfilling intellectual reality. | `social_psych` |
| 777 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1964 | The Kitty Genovese Murder: Triggering Bystander Research | New York City Police Reports | Stabbing murder in Queens with passive witnesses prompts Darley and Latane to investigate bystander apathy. | `social_psych` |
| 778 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1964 | Victor Vroom: Work and Motivation VIE Expectancy Theory | Victor H. Vroom, Carnegie Tech | Models workplace effort as a multiplicative function of Valence, Instrumentality, and Expectancy of work outcomes. | `industrial_org` |
| 779 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1964 | William Hamilton: Inclusive Fitness and Kin Selection Rule | William D. Hamilton, Imperial College | Solves Darwinian altruism paradox via Hamiltons Rule (rB > C): genes promote sacrifice when relatedness is high. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 780 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1965 | Diana Baumrind: Authoritative vs Authoritarian Parenting | Diana Baumrind, UC Berkeley | Classifies parenting styles along warmth and control axes, proving authoritative parenting fosters highest competence. | `developmental_psych` |
| 781 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1965 | Edward E. Jones & Keith Davis: Correspondent Inference | Edward E. Jones & Keith Davis | Models cognitive attribution: observers infer stable personality traits from actions perceived as freely chosen. | `social_psych` |
| 782 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1965 | Jose Delgado: Remote Stimoceiver Halts Charging Bull | Jose M.R. Delgado, Yale Medical School | Radio-controlled electrical stimulation of the caudate nucleus instantly halts a charging fighting bull in Cordoba ring. | `neuropsychology` |
| 783 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1965 | Joseph Weizenbaum: ELIZA Chatbot (First Psychotherapy AI) | Joseph Weizenbaum, MIT AI Lab | Rule-based ROGERIAN script mimics client-centered reflection, astonishing users who attribute deep empathy to computer. | `ai_psych` |
| 784 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1965 | Robert Zajonc: Social Facilitation Drive Theory Resolved | Robert B. Zajonc, Univ of Michigan | Spectator presence elevates arousal, facilitating dominant well-learned responses while impairing novel complex tasks. | `social_psych` |
| 785 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1965 | Ronald Melzack & Patrick Wall: Gate Control Theory of Pain | Ronald Melzack & Patrick Wall, MIT | Proves spinal dorsal horn neural gate modulates physical pain signals via descending cognitive and emotional inputs. | `neuropsychology` |
| 786 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1966 | Freedman & Fraser: Foot-in-the-Door Compliance Technique | Jonathan Freedman & Scott Fraser, Stanford | Homeowners agreeing to place a tiny safe-driving window sign are four times more likely to accept a massive billboard. | `social_psych` |
| 787 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1966 | Garcia & Koelling: Bright-Noisy Water Taste Aversion | John Garcia & Robert Koelling, UCLA | Rats associate nausea exclusively with taste and shock with audio-visual cues, proving evolutionary preparedness. | `behaviorism` |
| 788 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1966 | Julian Rotter: Locus of Control & Internal Expectancies | Julian B. Rotter, Psych Monographs | Publishes definitive 29-item I-E scale measuring individual differences in perceived personal efficacy and fatalism. | `social_psych` |
| 789 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1966 | Peter Wason: The 2-4-6 Hypothesis Testing Task | Peter Wason, Univ College London | Proves individuals systematically test hypotheses by seeking confirming evidence rather than attempting falsification. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 790 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1966 | Saul Sternberg: High-Speed Exhaustive Working Memory Scan | Saul Sternberg, Bell Telephone Labs | Reaction time rises 38 milliseconds per item in short-term memory sets, revealing serial exhaustive memory scanning. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 791 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1966 | William Masters & Virginia Johnson: Human Sexual Response | William Masters & Virginia Johnson, St. Louis | Recording of 10,000 sexual cycles maps human sexual response: excitement, plateau, orgasm, and resolution. | `clinical_psych` |
| 792 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Aaron Beck: Depression: Clinical and Theoretical Aspects | Aaron T. Beck, Univ of Pennsylvania | Full clinical monograph laying the foundation of Cognitive Therapy, replacing Freudian retroflected anger models. | `clinical_psych` |
| 793 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Hans Eysenck: The Biological Basis of Personality Arousal | Hans J. Eysenck, Maudsley Hospital | Links introversion-extraversion to baseline ascending reticular activating system (ARAS) cortical arousal thresholds. | `neuropsychology` |
| 794 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Harold Kelley: Attribution Theory in Social Psychology | Harold Kelley, Univ of California | Models how observers evaluate consensus, distinctiveness, and consistency across situations to make attributions. | `social_psych` |
| 795 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Hilary Putnam: Psychological Predicates & Functionalism | Hilary Putnam, Harvard University | Argues mental states are defined by functional causal roles rather than hardware, enabling multiple realizability. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 796 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Holmes & Rahe: Social Readjustment Rating Stress Scale | Thomas Holmes & Richard Rahe, Univ of Wash | Quantifies 43 major life changes in Life Change Units, proving cumulative stress predicts physical illness onset. | `clinical_psych` |
| 797 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Martin Seligman & Steven Maier: Learned Helplessness in Dogs | Martin Seligman & Steven Maier, Penn | Dogs subjected to inescapable electric shocks fail to escape when barrier is removed, modeling clinical depression. | `clinical_psych` |
| 798 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Stanley Milgram: The Small World Experiment (Six Degrees) | Stanley Milgram, Harvard University | Chains of forwarded letters between random individuals demonstrate humanity is linked by approximately six steps. | `social_psych` |
| 799 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1967 | Ulric Neisser: Cognitive Psychology Textbook Published | Ulric Neisser, Cornell University | Establishes scientific study of mental processes, sensory intake, memory storage, and cognitive retrieval. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 800 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | APA Publishes DSM-II: 182 Diagnostic Categories | American Psychiatric Association, DC | Revises diagnostic manual, expanding categories while maintaining psychoanalytic distinction of neurosis vs psychosis. | `clinical_psych` |
| 801 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | Edwin Locke: Toward a Theory of Task Motivation & Goals | Edwin A. Locke, Univ of Maryland | Proves specific, challenging quantitative goals yield vastly superior task performance over vague do-your-best prompts. | `industrial_org` |
| 802 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | John Darley & Bibb Latane: Bystander Intervention in Crisis | John Darley & Bibb Latane, Columbia Univ | Laboratory smoke-filled room and epileptic seizure trials prove diffusion of responsibility inhibits helping. | `social_psych` |
| 803 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | Peter Wason: The Four-Card Selection Task Formulation | Peter Wason, Univ College London | Logical deduction test (A, D, 4, 7) proves humans suffer severe cognitive bias, failing modus tollens refutation. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 804 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | Richard Atkinson & Richard Shiffrin: Multi-Store Memory | Richard Atkinson & Richard Shiffrin, Stanford | Defines cognitive structural architecture: Sensory Memory -> Short-Term Store (rehearsal buffer) -> Long-Term Store. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 805 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | Robert Rosenthal: Pygmalion in the Classroom Book Published | Robert Rosenthal & Lenore Jacobson | Documents transformative impact of high teacher expectations on elementary student standardized test performance. | `developmental_psych` |
| 806 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1968 | Walter Mischel: Personality and Assessment Situationism | Walter Mischel, Stanford University | Sparks the person-situation debate by showing cross-situational personality trait correlations rarely exceed r = 0.30. | `social_psych` |
| 807 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | Allan Paivio: Dual-Coding Theory of Cognition | Allan Paivio, Univ of Western Ontario | Dual-coding theory: human memory processes visual images and linguistic information in separate parallel systems. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 808 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | Arthur Jensen: How Much Can We Boost IQ and Scholastic | Arthur Jensen, Harvard Educational Review | Controversial paper alleging genetic differences in racial IQ, sparking massive scientific protests and condemnation. | `social_psych` |
| 809 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | Collins & Quillian: Hierarchical Semantic Network Memory | Allan Collins & M. Ross Quillian, BBN | Sentence verification latencies (canary is yellow vs animal) prove concepts are stored in hierarchical taxonomy nodes. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 810 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | Elisabeth Kubler-Ross: Five Stages of Grief Framework | Elisabeth Kubler-Ross, Chicago | Formulates the five psychological stages of grief and bereavement: Denial, Anger, Bargaining, Depression, Acceptance. | `clinical_psych` |
| 811 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | George Miller: Giving Psychology Away APA Presidential | George A. Miller, Washington DC | Urges psychologists to translate scientific findings into public knowledge to advance human welfare. | `positive_psych` |
| 812 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | John Bowlby: Attachment (Attachment and Loss Volume 1) | John Bowlby, Tavistock Clinic London | Landmark monograph integrating evolutionary survival drives with infant attachment behaviors and maternal safe havens. | `developmental_psych` |
| 813 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | Mary Ainsworth: The Strange Situation Attachment Protocol | Mary Ainsworth & Barbara Wittig, Johns Hopkins | Standardized 20-minute separation-reunion paradigm classifying Secure, Insecure-Avoidant, and Ambivalent infant styles. | `developmental_psych` |
| 814 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1969 | Paul Ekman & Wallace Friesen: Universal Facial Expressions | Paul Ekman & Wallace Friesen, New Guinea | Isolated Fore tribe members identify universal facial expressions of 6 basic emotions, confirming Darwin. | `cultural_psych` |
| 815 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Abraham Maslow: Motivation and Personality Second Edition | Abraham Maslow, Menlo Park CA | Expands self-actualization theory with self-transcendence, published shortly before his sudden death in 1970. | `positive_psych` |
| 816 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Alexander Luria: Traumatic Aphasia and Neuropsychological | Alexander Luria, Moscow Univ | Classic monograph synthesizing Soviet neuropsychology: functional systems, compensatory neural plasticity, and recovery. | `neuropsychology` |
| 817 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | David McClelland: The Two Faces of Power in Management | David McClelland, Harvard Business Review | Distinguishes personalized power (exploitative domination) from socialized power (empowering organizational teams). | `industrial_org` |
| 818 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Gordon Gallup: Mirror Self-Recognition Test in Chimpanzees | Gordon Gallup Jr., Tulane University | Chimpanzees touch red paint marked on their anesthetized eyebrows in mirror, proving animal self-awareness. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 819 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Henri Tajfel: Minimal Group Paradigm Experiments in Bristol | Henri Tajfel, Bristol University | Assigning schoolboys to arbitrary groups based on painting preference provokes immediate in-group favoritism. | `social_psych` |
| 820 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Irvin Yalom: The Theory and Practice of Group Psychotherapy | Irvin D. Yalom, Stanford Medical | Definitive text identifying 11 therapeutic factors in group therapy: universality, interpersonal learning, catharsis. | `clinical_psych` |
| 821 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Saul Kripke: Naming and Necessity Lectures at Princeton | Saul Kripke, Princeton University | Refutes identity theory: mental states cannot be identical to physical brain states (multiple realizability). | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 822 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Stanley Smith Stevens: Psychophysics Scaling and Stevens | Stanley Smith Stevens, Harvard University | Power law (P = k * I^n) replaces Fechner, accurately predicting sensation for brightness, loudness, and shock. | `experimental_psych` |
| 823 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Takeo Doi: The Anatomy of Dependence (Japanese Amae) | Takeo Doi, Univ of Tokyo | Analyzes Amae—the comforting expectation of indulgent maternal care—as the foundational keystone of Japanese psyche. | `cultural_psych` |
| 824 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Thomas O. Nelson & Thomas Narens: Metacognition Framework | Thomas O. Nelson & Thomas Narens | Pioneers experimental study of monitoring and controlling ones own cognitive performance and metamemory. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 825 | 7. Gestalt & Humanistic Wave | 1970 | Walter Mischel: Stanford Marshmallow Delay of Gratification | Walter Mischel & Ebbe Ebbesen, Stanford | Children who resist eating marshmallow for 15 minutes display superior academic and social resilience decades later. | `developmental_psych` |

## 8. Cognitive Revolution (#826 – #1025)

| # | Era | Year / Period | Topic Title | Key Figure(s) / Institution | 1-Sentence Core Summary | Subfield |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 826 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Allan Paivio: Imagery and Verbal Processes Monograph | Allan Paivio, Univ of Western Ontario | Foundational dual-coding theory: verbal and non-verbal symbolic systems operate independently and additively. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 827 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | B.F. Skinner: Beyond Freedom and Dignity Published | B.F. Skinner, Harvard University | Controversial treatise arguing concepts of autonomous freedom and moral dignity impede scientific design of culture. | `behaviorism` |
| 828 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Donald Winnicott: Playing and Reality Transitional Objects | Donald Winnicott, Tavistock Clinic | Explores the intermediate potential space between inner psychic reality and external world via security blankets. | `psychoanalysis` |
| 829 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Edward Deci: Intrinsic Motivation Undermined by Extrinsic | Edward L. Deci, Univ of Rochester | Overjustification effect: paying students to solve puzzles paradoxically diminishes subsequent intrinsic interest. | `social_psych` |
| 830 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Henri Tajfel: Social Identity Theory Formulated | Henri Tajfel, Bristol University | Proves social categorization alone triggers in-group favoritism and out-group discrimination. | `social_psych` |
| 831 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Philip Zimbardo: The Stanford Prison Experiment | Philip Zimbardo, Stanford Univ | Mock prison study aborted after 6 days as normal students act as sadistic guards and traumatized prisoners. | `social_psych` |
| 832 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Robert Trivers: Evolution of Reciprocal Altruism Theory | Robert L. Trivers, Harvard University | Mathematical evolutionary model explaining how cooperation evolves among unrelated individuals via repeated reciprocity. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 833 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Roger Shepard & Jacqueline Metzler: Mental Rotation | Roger Shepard & Jacqueline Metzler | Reaction time increases linearly with angular disparity, proving minds mentally rotate 3D spatial representations. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 834 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Simon & Chase: Skill in Chess Perceptual Chunking Study | Herbert Simon & William Chase, CMU | Chess grandmasters recall board positions via 50,000 stored perceptual chunks but match novices on random boards. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 835 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1971 | Terry Winograd: SHRDLU Natural Language AI Program | Terry Winograd, MIT AI Lab | Early artificial intelligence system understands English commands and manipulates objects in simulated block world. | `ai_psych` |
| 836 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Amos Tversky & Daniel Kahneman: Availability Heuristic | Amos Tversky & Daniel Kahneman | Demonstrates frequency judgments depend on the cognitive ease with which relevant instances come to mind. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 837 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Daryl Bem: Self-Perception Theory of Attitude Change | Daryl J. Bem, Stanford University | Individuals infer their own internal attitudes by observing their overt external behavior like an outside observer. | `social_psych` |
| 838 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Duval & Wicklund: A Theory of Objective Self-Awareness | Shelley Duval & Robert Wicklund, Texas | Mirrors and cameras focus attention inward, triggering self-evaluation against internal moral and performance standards. | `social_psych` |
| 839 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Endel Tulving: Episodic and Semantic Memory Dichotomy | Endel Tulving, Academic Press | Splits declarative memory into episodic (personal autobiographical events) and semantic (general world knowledge). | `cognitive_psych` |
| 840 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Fergus Craik & Robert Lockhart: Levels of Processing Model | Fergus Craik & Robert Lockhart, Toronto | Deeper semantic analysis during encoding yields significantly superior retention than shallow perceptual processing. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 841 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Hubert Dreyfus: What Computers Cant Do AI Critique | Hubert L. Dreyfus, UC Berkeley | Phenomenological critique arguing human intelligence relies on embodied intuition rather than formal symbolic rules. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 842 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Irving Janis: Victims of Groupthink Social Pathology | Irving Janis, Yale University | Analyzes Bay of Pigs and Pearl Harbor: cohesive group loyalty and concurrence-seeking suppress critical appraisal. | `social_psych` |
| 843 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | John Money: David Reimer Case and Gender Socialization | John Money, Johns Hopkins Hospital | Advocates surgical sex reassignment after circumcision accident, sparking tragic controversy on gender innate identity. | `developmental_psych` |
| 844 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Martin Seligman: Phobias and Preparedness in Conditioning | Martin E.P. Seligman, Univ of Pennsylvania | Explains why humans rapidly acquire phobias of ancestral threats (snakes, heights) rather than modern dangers (cars). | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 845 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Paul Ekman: Darwin and Facial Expression Centennial Review | Paul Ekman, Academic Press | Synthesizes global cross-cultural data proving six basic emotions possess pancultural universal facial signatures. | `cultural_psych` |
| 846 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Robert Rescorla & Allan Wagner: Rescorla-Wagner | Robert Rescorla & Allan Wagner | Mathematical learning model: conditioning depends on prediction error (discrepancy between expected and actual outcome). | `behaviorism` |
| 847 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1972 | Walter Mischel: Cognitive Mechanisms in Delay of Reward | Walter Mischel et al., Stanford | Teaches children cognitive distraction strategies (cool transformations), vastly extending delayed gratification. | `developmental_psych` |
| 848 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | APA Removes Homosexuality from DSM Mental Disorders | American Psychiatric Association, DC | APA Trustees vote to remove homosexuality from DSM-II following intense protests and Evelyn Hookers empirical data. | `clinical_psych` |
| 849 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Alexander Luria: The Working Brain and Neuropsychology | Alexander Luria, Moscow University | Definitive functional system model dividing brain into three blocks: arousal, sensory processing, and programming. | `neuropsychology` |
| 850 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Amos Tversky & Daniel Kahneman: Representativeness Heuristic | Amos Tversky & Daniel Kahneman | Shows people judge likelihood by similarity to mental prototypes, committing base-rate fallacy and conjunction fallacy. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 851 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Bliss & Lomo: Discovery of Long-Term Potentiation (LTP) | Tim Bliss & Terje Lomo, Oslo & London | High-frequency stimulation of rabbit hippocampal pathways produces enduring synaptic strengthening, modeling memory. | `neuropsychology` |
| 852 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Darley & Batson: From Jerusalem to Jericho Good Samaritan | John Darley & C. Daniel Batson, Princeton | Theological seminary students in a hurry to deliver a Good Samaritan sermon step right over a coughing victim. | `social_psych` |
| 853 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | David Rosenhan: On Being Sane in Insane Places | David Rosenhan, Science Journal | Pseudo-patients feigning single auditory symptom are admitted to 12 psychiatric hospitals; normal acts viewed as crazy. | `clinical_psych` |
| 854 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Endel Tulving & Donald Thomson: Encoding Specificity | Endel Tulving & Donald Thomson | Memory retrieval is optimal when cognitive retrieval cues closely match conditions present during initial encoding. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 855 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Eric Kandel: Synaptic Habituation in Aplysia Californica | Eric R. Kandel, Columbia University | Traces short-term habituation and sensitization to presynaptic transmitter release changes in marine snail reflexes. | `neuropsychology` |
| 856 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Ernest Becker: The Denial of Death Masterpiece | Ernest Becker, Pulitzer Prize | Existential psychoanalysis arguing human culture and ego defenses are elaborate symbolic shields against death terror. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 857 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1973 | Mark Lepper: Hidden Costs of Reward in Preschoolers | Mark R. Lepper & David Greene, Stanford | Drawing with felt-tip markers: children given expected good player certificate show sharp drop in free-play drawing. | `developmental_psych` |

| 980 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1985 | Robert Sternberg: Beyond IQ: A Triarchic Theory of Human | Robert J. Sternberg, Cambridge Press | Triarchic framework: Analytical intelligence (academic), Creative intelligence (novelty), and Practical intelligence. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 981 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1985 | Simon Baron-Cohen, Alan Leslie & Uta Frith: Sally-Anne Test | Baron-Cohen, Leslie & Frith, London | Demonstrates autistic children specifically fail false-belief Theory of Mind tasks, introducing mindblindness concept. | `developmental_psych` |
| 982 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | Albert Bandura: Social Foundations of Thought and Action | Albert Bandura, Prentice-Hall | Definitive social cognitive theory: triadic reciprocal determinism linking cognitive factors, behavior, and environment. | `social_psych` |
| 983 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | David Clark: A Cognitive Approach to Panic Disorder | David M. Clark, Oxford University | Proposes panic attacks result from catastrophic misinterpretation of benign bodily sensations like racing heartbeats. | `clinical_psych` |
| 984 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | David Rumelhart & James McClelland: Parallel Distributed | Rumelhart, McClelland & PDP Group | Connectionist manifesto establishing neural network models and backpropagation algorithm in cognitive science. | `ai_psych` |
| 985 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | Greenberg, Pyszczynski & Solomon: Terror Management Theory | Jeff Greenberg, Tom Pyszczynski & Solomon | Mortality salience prompts individuals to defend cultural worldviews and bolster self-esteem to buffer death terror. | `social_psych` |
| 986 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | John Sweller: Cognitive Load Theory in Instructional | John Sweller, Univ of New South Wales | Identifies intrinsic, extraneous, and germane cognitive load, optimizing instructional design for working memory. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 987 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | Paul Churchland: Matter and Consciousness Neurophilosophy | Paul M. Churchland, MIT Press | Advocates eliminative materialism: folk psychological concepts (beliefs, desires) will be eliminated by neuroscience. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 988 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | Renee Baillargeon: Object Permanence in 4-Month-Old Infants | Renee Baillargeon, Univ of Illinois | Drawbridge violation-of-expectation paradigm proves infants grasp object permanence months earlier than Piaget claimed. | `developmental_psych` |
| 989 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | Richard Lazarus & Susan Folkman: Model of Stress & Coping | Richard S. Lazarus & Susan Folkman | Stress transaction model: primary appraisal evaluates threat; secondary appraisal evaluates coping resources. | `clinical_psych` |
| 990 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1986 | Robert Sternberg: A Triangular Theory of Love | Robert J. Sternberg, Psychological Review | Triangular theory of love: Intimacy (emotional), Passion (motivational), and Commitment (cognitive). | `social_psych` |
| 991 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | APA Publishes DSM-III-R: Diagnostic Criteria Revised | American Psychiatric Association, DC | Revises DSM-III criteria, clarifying diagnostic thresholds and incorporating emerging psychiatric field trials. | `clinical_psych` |
| 992 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Cindy Hazan & Phillip Shaver: Romantic Love Conceptualized | Cindy Hazan & Phillip Shaver, JPSP | Applies Bowlby-Ainsworth infant attachment styles to adult romantic relationships: Secure, Avoidant, and Anxious. | `social_psych` |
| 993 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Daniel Schacter: Implicit Memory History and Performance | Daniel L. Schacter, Univ of Toronto | Formalizes implicit memory as non-conscious facilitation on tests not requiring intentional recollection of episodes. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 994 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Daniel Wegner: Paradoxical Effects of Thought Suppression | Daniel M. Wegner et al., JPSP | Ironic process theory: attempting not to think of a white bear triggers rebound effect flooding mind with bear thoughts. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 995 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | E. Tory Higgins: Self-Discrepancy Theory of Affect | E. Tory Higgins, New York University | Actual-Ideal self discrepancies trigger dejection and sadness, whereas Actual-Ought discrepancies trigger agitation. | `social_psych` |
| 996 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Francine Shapiro: Discovery of Eye Movement Desensitization | Francine Shapiro, San Jose CA | Discovers rapid bilateral saccadic eye movements during recall of distressing memories defuses acute emotional trauma. | `clinical_psych` |
| 997 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Irving Biederman: Recognition-by-Components (Geon Theory) | Irving Biederman, Univ of Minnesota | Explains human 3D visual object recognition via 36 volumetric geometric primitives called geons (cylinders, cones). | `cognitive_psych` |
| 998 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Leda Cosmides & John Tooby: Evolutionary Psychology Program | Leda Cosmides & John Tooby, UCSB | Founds modern evolutionary psychology: human minds evolved domain-specific computational modules in Pleistocene savanna. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 999 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1987 | Prozac (Fluoxetine) Receives FDA Approval: SSRI Revolution | US FDA & Eli Lilly, Indianapolis | First selective serotonin reuptake inhibitor approved for clinical depression, transforming outpatient treatment. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1000 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | American Psychological Society (APS) Founded by Academic | Charles Kiesler & 400 Researchers | Founds APS to champion basic experimental psychological science separate from commercial clinical practitioner lobbying. | `experimental_psych` |
| 1001 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | Bernard Baars: Cognitive Consciousness (Global Workspace) | Bernard J. Baars, Cambridge Press | Global Workspace Theory: consciousness functions like a theater stage broadcasting focal info to unconscious modules. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1002 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | Claude Steele: The Psychology of Self-Affirmation | Claude M. Steele, Univ of Michigan | Affirming core personal values restores global ego integrity, reducing defensive rationalization under threat. | `social_psych` |
| 1003 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | Dan McAdams: Power, Intimacy, and the Life Story Identity | Dan P. McAdams, Northwestern University | Formulates the Life Story Model of adult identity: people integrate their lives into evolving autobiographical myths. | `social_psych` |
| 1004 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | George Valliant: Adaptation to Life 40-Year Longitudinal | George E. Vaillant, Harvard Grant Study | Follows Harvard men across four decades, proving mature psychological defenses (humor, sublimation) predict thriving. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1005 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | Michael Posner & Marcus Raichle: PET Imaging of Language | Michael Posner & Marcus Raichle, Nature | First PET functional neuroimaging mapping distinct brain areas activated during reading, listening, and words. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1006 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | Patricia Churchland: Neurophilosophy: Toward a Unified | Patricia Smith Churchland, MIT Press | Pioneering treatise bridging analytic philosophy of mind with empirical neurobiology, synaptic plasticity, and networks. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 1007 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1988 | Strack, Martin & Stepper: Facial Feedback Pen-in-Mouth | Fritz Strack, Leonard Martin & Stepper | Holding a pen between the teeth to activate smiling muscles makes cartoons seem funnier without emotional awareness. | `experimental_psych` |
| 1008 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Carol Ryff: Six-Factor Model of Psychological Well-Being | Carol D. Ryff, Univ of Wisconsin-Madison | Defines eudaimonic wellbeing across six dimensions: autonomy, mastery, growth, positive relations, purpose, acceptance. | `positive_psych` |
| 1009 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | David Buss: Sex Differences in Human Mate Preferences: 37 | David M. Buss, BBS Journal | 10,047 participants across 37 cultures confirm evolutionary predictions: men value youth, women value resources. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1010 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Elizabeth Loftus: Memory Traitor: The Repressed Memory Wars | Elizabeth Loftus & Katherine Ketcham | Challenges therapists manufacturing false recovered memories of childhood satanic abuse via hypnotic suggestion. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1011 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Francine Shapiro: First Clinical Trial of EMDR for PTSD | Francine Shapiro, Journal of Traumatic Stress | Controlled clinical trial shows eye movement desensitization dramatically reduces traumatic distress in combat veterans. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1012 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Harry Triandis: The Self and Social Behavior in Cultures | Harry C. Triandis, Univ of Illinois | Maps how Individualist vs Collectivist cultural syndromes shape private, public, and collective self-sampling rates. | `cultural_psych` |
| 1013 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Markus & Kitayama: Dynamic Self-Concept Schema Model | Hazel Rose Markus & Shinobu Kitayama | Pioneers cross-cultural psychology: Western independent autonomous self vs East Asian interdependent connected self. | `cultural_psych` |
| 1014 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Patricia Devine: Stereotypes and Prejudice Controlled Model | Patricia G. Devine, Univ of Wisconsin | Dissociates automatic cultural stereotype activation from intentional controlled inhibition in low-prejudice people. | `social_psych` |
| 1015 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Richard Shweder: Cultural Psychology: What Is It? Landmark | Richard A. Shweder, Chicago | Declares psyche and culture make each other up: universal psychology is impossible without examining cultural context. | `cultural_psych` |
| 1016 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1989 | Susan Nolen-Hoeksema: Sex Differences in Unipolar | Susan Nolen-Hoeksema, Stanford Univ | Response styles theory: women ruminate on depressive distress while men distract, explaining female depression ratios. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1017 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Jerome Bruner: Acts of Meaning and the Narrative Turn | Jerome Bruner, Harvard Univ Press | Reclaims cognitive revolution from computer metaphors, arguing the human mind organizes experience through narrative. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1018 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Locke & Latham: A Theory of Goal Setting and Performance | Edwin A. Locke & Gary P. Latham | Synthesizes 25 years of 400 workplace studies proving challenging, specific goals with feedback maximize productivity. | `industrial_org` |
| 1019 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Mihaly Csikszentmihalyi: Flow: The Psychology of Optimal | Mihaly Csikszentmihalyi, Harper & Row | Bestselling masterwork formalizing conditions for autotelic flow: clear goals, instant feedback, and deep focus. | `positive_psych` |
| 1020 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Paul Costa & Robert McCrae: The NEO Personality Inventory | Paul T. Costa & Robert R. McCrae, NIH | Definitive 5-factor model of human personality: Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, Neuroticism. | `social_psych` |
| 1021 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Peter Salovey & John D. Mayer: Emotional Intelligence | Peter Salovey & John D. Mayer, Imagination | Coins Emotional Intelligence: the ability to perceive, integrate, understand, and regulate emotions to promote growth. | `social_psych` |
| 1022 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Roger Shepard: Mind Sights Perceptual Illusions Monograph | Roger N. Shepard, Stanford University | Demonstrates Shepard Tables and universal perceptual laws governing internal mental representation of 3D space. | `experimental_psych` |
| 1023 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Roy Baumeister: Escape From Self Theory of Suicide | Roy F. Baumeister, Case Western Reserve | Models suicide as an escalating six-step cognitive deconstruction to escape intolerable self-awareness after failure. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1024 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Seiji Ogawa: Discovery of BOLD Contrast (Birth of fMRI) | Seiji Ogawa, Bell Laboratories NJ | Discovers blood-oxygen-level-dependent (BOLD) MRI signal, creating functional neuroimaging to revolutionize psychology. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1025 | 8. Cognitive Revolution | 1990 | Steven Pinker & Paul Bloom: Natural Language and Natural | Steven Pinker & Paul Bloom, BBS | Argues human language faculty is a complex biological adaptation evolved by Darwinian selection for communication. | `evolutionary_psych` |

## 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era (#1026 – #1240)

| # | Era | Year / Period | Topic Title | Key Figure(s) / Institution | 1-Sentence Core Summary | Subfield |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1026 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Daniel Dennett: Consciousness Explained Published | Daniel C. Dennett, Tufts University | Multiple drafts model of mind: consciousness is not a central Cartesian theater but parallel neural edits. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 1027 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Ellen Bialystok: Bilingual Executive Function Advantages | Ellen Bialystok, York University | Proves lifelong bilingualism enhances executive control, task switching, and delays dementia onset. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1028 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Giacomo Rizzolatti: Discovery of Premotor Mirror Neurons | Giacomo Rizzolatti & Vittorio Gallese | Discovers neurons in macaque F5 area firing identically during executing and observing hand grasping. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1029 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Hazel Markus & Shinobu Kitayama: Culture and the Self | Markus & Kitayama, Psychological Review | Pioneering cross-cultural psychology: independent Western self vs interdependent East Asian connected self. | `cultural_psych` |
| 1030 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | John Cacioppo: Social Neuroscience Term Coined | John Cacioppo & Gary Berntson, American Psych | Integrates social psychology and biological mechanisms, founding interdisciplinary social neuroscience. | `social_psych` |
| 1031 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Larry Jacoby: The Process Dissociation Procedure in Memory | Larry L. Jacoby, McMaster University | Uses inclusion-exclusion opposition tasks to algebraically separate conscious recollection from automatic familiarity. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1032 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Marsha Linehan: Randomized Controlled Trial of DBT | Marsha M. Linehan, Arch Gen Psychiatry | First clinical trial proving Dialectical Behavior Therapy halves suicide attempts in borderline patients. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1033 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Meyer & Allen: Three-Component Organizational Commitment | John P. Meyer & Natalie J. Allen, Western Ontario | Distinguishes Affective commitment (desire), Continuance commitment (cost), and Normative commitment (obligation). | `industrial_org` |
| 1034 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Miller & Rollnick: Motivational Interviewing Clinical Text | William R. Miller & Stephen Rollnick | Client-centered counseling method for resolving ambivalence and eliciting intrinsic behavior change talk in addiction. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1035 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Patricia Kuhl: Native Language Magnet Effect in Infants | Patricia K. Kuhl, Univ of Washington | Six-month-old infants warp acoustic phonetic perception around native vowel prototypes before speaking a single word. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1036 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1991 | Robin Dunbar: Dunbars Number 150 Social Brain Limit | Robin Dunbar, University College London | Correlates primate neocortex volume with group size, calculating natural human social group limit at 150. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1037 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | David Buss: Sex Differences in Romantic Infidelity Jealousy | David M. Buss et al., Psych Science | Physiological telemetry shows men show higher autonomic distress to sexual cheating; women to emotional. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1038 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Gerald Edelman: Neural Darwinism Computational Models | Gerald M. Edelman, Basic Books | Models cognitive brain development as somatic selection competing across degenerate neuronal groups. | `ai_psych` |
| 1039 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Goodale & Milner: Separate Visual Pathways for Action | Melvyn A. Goodale & A. David Milner | Patient D.F. with visual form agnosia cannot judge slot angle yet smoothly posts a letter through it via dorsal stream. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1040 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Jerome Barkow, Leda Cosmides & John Tooby: The Adapted Mind | Cosmides, Tooby & Barkow, Oxford | Foundational evolutionary psychology manifesto: human mind consists of domain-specific evolved modules. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1041 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | John Gottman: The Four Horsemen Predicting Marital Divorce | John Gottman, Univ of Washington | Observational coding of criticism, contempt, defensiveness, and stonewalling predicts divorce with 93% accuracy. | `social_psych` |
| 1042 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Judith Herman: Trauma and Recovery & Complex PTSD | Judith Lewis Herman, Harvard Medical School | Unifies combat veterans and domestic abuse survivors, introducing Complex PTSD from prolonged coercive captivity. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1043 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | K. Anders Ericsson: Deliberate Practice in Expert | K. Anders Ericsson, Ralf Krampe & Tesch | 10,000-hour deliberate practice rule: elite mastery requires focused, effortful practice with feedback. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1044 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Karen Wynn: Addition and Subtraction by Human Infants | Karen Wynn, University of Arizona | Five-month-old infants stare longer at impossible 1+1=1 puppet outcomes, proving innate numerical magnitude tracking. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1045 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Kenneth Dodge: Social Information Processing in Child | Kenneth A. Dodge & Nicky Crick, Duke | Models youth social cognition: aggressive children misinterpret neutral peer cues as hostile provocations. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1046 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Raymond, Shapiro & Arnell: The Attentional Blink Discovery | Jane Raymond, Kimron Shapiro & Karen Arnell | Identifying a first target in rapid visual streams blinds conscious detection of a second target 200-500ms later. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1047 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Robert McCrae & Paul Costa: Five-Factor Personality Theory | Robert McCrae & Paul Costa, Guilford | Systematizes NEO-PI-R personality architecture, demonstrating cross-cultural biological universality of traits. | `social_psych` |
| 1048 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1992 | Steven Pinker: The Language Instinct Evolutionary Monograph | Steven Pinker, MIT & HarperCollins | Argues language is an innate human biological adaptation evolved by natural selection, popularized for millions. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1049 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Ambady & Rosenthal: Half a Minute Thin Slices of Behavior | Nalini Ambady & Robert Rosenthal, Harvard | Silent 2-second and 10-second video clips of college teachers accurately predict end-of-semester student evaluations. | `social_psych` |
| 1050 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Hammer & Champy: Reengineering and Worker Burnout | Michael Hammer & James Champy | Corporate downsizing and reengineering trends trigger widespread occupational burnout and survivor guilt. | `industrial_org` |
| 1051 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Howard Gardner: Multiple Intelligences Theory in Practice | Howard Gardner, Basic Books | Translates multiple intelligences into educational curricula, individualized assessment, and project classrooms. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1052 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Ivar Lovaas: Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) in Autism | O. Ivar Lovaas, UCLA Clinic | Intensive 40-hour operant conditioning program achieves mainstream educational placement in 47% of autistic youth. | `behaviorism` |
| 1053 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Kahneman & Fredrickson: Duration Neglect & Peak-End Rule | Daniel Kahneman, Barbara Fredrickson et al. | Subjects prefer a 90-second ice-water trial with a slightly warmer final 30 seconds over a shorter 60-second immersion. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1054 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Kenneth Craik & Donald MacKinnon: Berkeley IPAR Creativity | IPAR Archive, UC Berkeley | Summarizes 40 years of living assessment studies measuring creative architects, writers, and scientists. | `positive_psych` |
| 1055 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Marsha Linehan: Cognitive-Behavioral Treatment of BPD Manual | Marsha M. Linehan, Guilford Press | Comprehensive manual for Dialectical Behavior Therapy (DBT): mindfulness, distress tolerance, emotion regulation. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1056 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Martin Daly & Margo Wilson: The Cinderella Effect | Martin Daly & Margo Wilson, McMaster | Evolutionary kinship data proves stepchildren face significantly higher risk of physical abuse than genetic offspring. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1057 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Peter Kramer: Listening to Prozac and Cosmetic | Peter D. Kramer, Viking Press | Bestseller examining ethical questions when SSRIs alter personality, confidence, and identity beyond depression. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1058 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Roy Baumeister: The Need to Belong Fundamental Human Motive | Roy F. Baumeister & Mark Leary | Reviews evidence showing interpersonal attachment and social belonging are pervasive fundamental human drives. | `social_psych` |
| 1059 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1993 | Terrie Moffitt: Adolescence-Limited vs Life-Course Crime | Terrie E. Moffitt, Univ of Wisconsin | Taxonomy distinguishing common adolescent peer delinquency from persistent life-course antisocial neuropathology. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1060 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | APA Publishes DSM-IV: Allen Frances Task Force Compendium | Allen Frances & APA Task Force, DC | Classifies 297 mental disorders using extensive empirical literature reviews and multi-center field trials. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1061 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Antonio Damasio: Descartess Error and Somatic Marker | Antonio Damasio, Putnam | Proves emotional bodily signals (somatic markers) are indispensable for rational human decision-making. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1062 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Bechara & Damasio: Iowa Gambling Task Somatic Markers | Antoine Bechara & Antonio Damasio, Iowa | Ventromedial prefrontal lesion patients fail to generate anticipatory skin-conductance warnings before risky choices. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1063 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | David Chalmers: Facing the Hard Problem of Consciousness | David J. Chalmers, Univ of Arizona | Defines the Hard Problem: why and how physical brain processes give rise to subjective conscious experience. | `philosophy_of_mind` |
| 1064 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Esther Thelen & Linda Smith: Dynamic Systems Development | Esther Thelen & Linda B. Smith, Indiana | Models infant motor and cognitive growth as self-organizing emergent coordination rather than pre-programmed stages. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1065 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Karl Friston: Statistical Parametric Mapping (SPM) in fMRI | Karl J. Friston, Wellcome Trust UCL | Establishes voxel-wise general linear modeling and Gaussian random fields as the standard for neuroimaging analysis. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1066 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Michael Gazzaniga: The Cognitive Neurosciences First Edition | Michael S. Gazzaniga, MIT Press | Monumental 1,400-page handbook defining cognitive neuroscience as the unified science of brain and mind. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1067 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Paul Ekman & Richard Davidson: The Nature of Emotion | Paul Ekman & Richard Davidson, Oxford | Brings together leading emotion researchers to map physiological, cognitive, and communicative emotional facets. | `experimental_psych` |
| 1068 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1994 | Richard Herrnstein & Charles Murray: The Bell Curve | Richard Herrnstein & Charles Murray | Controversial bestseller alleging genetic IQ differences govern social stratification, igniting massive protests. | `social_psych` |
| 1069 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Ceci & Bruck: Jeopardy in the Courtroom Child Witness | Stephen J. Ceci & Maggie Bruck, Cornell | Demonstrates repeated suggestive forensic interviewing implants elaborate false memories in preschool children. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1070 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Claude Steele & Joshua Aronson: Stereotype Threat | Claude M. Steele & Joshua Aronson, Stanford | Black students score lower on GRE verbal tests when racial stereotypes are primed, proving stereotype threat. | `social_psych` |
| 1071 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Daniel Goleman: Emotional Intelligence Bestselling | Daniel Goleman, Bantam Books | Popularizes EQ: self-awareness, impulse control, empathy, and social skill are better predictors of life success. | `social_psych` |
| 1072 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Ed Diener & Eunkook Suh: National Differences in Well-Being | Ed Diener, Eunkook Suh, Smith & Shao | Cross-national comparison shows individualistic wealth and cultural norms differentially shape life satisfaction. | `cultural_psych` |
| 1073 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Elizabeth Loftus: The Lost in the Mall False Memory | Elizabeth F. Loftus & Jacqueline Pickrell | Successfully implants rich false childhood memory of being lost in shopping mall in 25% of healthy participants. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1074 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Giacomo Rizzolatti: Premotor Cortex Localization of Mirror | Rizzolatti et al., Experimental Brain Research | Proves premotor mirror neurons discharge both during goal-directed action and action visual observation. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1075 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Joseph LeDoux: The Emotional Brain and Dual Fear Pathways | Joseph LeDoux, New York University | Maps fast low road from thalamus directly to amygdala vs slow high road through visual cortex for fear. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1076 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Kwang-Kuo Hwang: Face and Favor in Chinese Social Psychology | Kwang-Kuo Hwang, National Taiwan Univ | Analyzes Confucian social mechanisms of Mianzi (face), Renqing (favor), and Guanxi (interpersonal power). | `cultural_psych` |
| 1077 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Roediger & McDermott: The DRM False Memory Paradigm | Henry L. Roediger & Kathleen B. McDermott | Studying semantic associates (bed, rest, awake) induces 55% false recall of the non-presented critical lure (sleep). | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1078 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Rosalind Picard: Affective Computing at MIT Media Lab | Rosalind W. Picard, MIT Tech Report | Foundational manifesto urging computer systems to recognize, express, and model human emotional signals. | `ai_psych` |
| 1079 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1995 | Russell Barkley: Behavioral Inhibition and ADHD Executive | Russell A. Barkley, UMass Medical | Unified neuropsychological model: ADHD is fundamentally a deficit in behavioral inhibition and executive time sense. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1080 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Christina Maslach: Maslach Burnout Inventory Third Edition | Christina Maslach & Susan Jackson | Validates three core psychological burnout dimensions: emotional exhaustion, depersonalization, and reduced efficacy. | `industrial_org` |
| 1081 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | David Lykken & Auke Tellegen: Happiness is a Stochastic | David Lykken & Auke Tellegen, Psych Science | Minnesota twin study estimates 50% of subjective wellbeing is genetically determined, proposing set-point theory. | `positive_psych` |
| 1082 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Deep Blue Defeats Garry Kasparov in Single Game | IBM Deep Blue Team, Philadelphia | First chess game victory of computer program over reigning world champion, shifting AI psychology benchmarks. | `ai_psych` |
| 1083 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Frans de Waal: Good Natured: Origins of Morality in Primates | Frans de Waal, Harvard Univ Press | Documents empathy, consolation, fairness, and reciprocal food sharing in chimpanzees and bonobos. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1084 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Gary Klein: Sources of Power: How People Make Decisions | Gary Klein, MIT Press | Recognition-Primed Decision (RPD) model: expert firefighters and pilots match patterns instantly without calculating. | `cognitive_psych` |
| 1085 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Geoffrey Miller: The Mating Mind and Sexual Selection | Geoffrey Miller, London School of Econ | Argues human humor, art, music, and morality evolved as courtship displays signaling genetic cognitive fitness. | `evolutionary_psych` |
| 1086 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | John Bargh, Mark Chen & Burrows: Automaticity of Behavior | John A. Bargh, Mark Chen & Lara Burrows, NYU | Unconsciously priming elderly stereotype words causes college students to walk more slowly down the hallway afterward. | `social_psych` |
| 1087 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Laura Carstensen: Socioemotional Selectivity Theory in Aging | Laura L. Carstensen, Stanford Univ | Aging individuals perceive shrinking time horizons, shifting priority to emotionally meaningful close relationships. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1088 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Saffran, Aslin & Newport: Statistical Learning by Infants | Jenny Saffran, Richard Aslin & Elissa Newport | Eight-month-old infants segment continuous nonsense speech streams using solely transitional syllable probabilities. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1089 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1996 | Vittorio Gallese: Action Recognition in Premotor Cortex | Vittorio Gallese et al., Brain Journal | Demonstrates mirror neurons provide direct neural simulation mechanism for understanding others actions. | `neuropsychology` |
| 1090 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1997 | David Clark & Adrian Wells: Cognitive Model of Social Phobia | David M. Clark & Adrian Wells, Oxford | Shows social anxiety persists via internal self-focused attention, negative observer imagery, and safety behaviors. | `clinical_psych` |
| 1091 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1997 | Deep Blue Defeats Garry Kasparov in Rematch Tournament | IBM Deep Blue Team, New York | Historic milestone: computer system wins 6-game match against world chess champion, validating heuristic search. | `ai_psych` |
| 1092 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1997 | Dermot Barnes-Holmes: Arbitrary Derived Relational | Dermot Barnes-Holmes, Cork Ireland | Empirical laboratory demonstration of relational framing forming symbolic cognitive equivalence without reinforcement. | `behaviorism` |
| 1093 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1997 | John Gottman: Why Marriages Succeed or Fail Mathematical | John M. Gottman, Simon & Schuster | Identifies the magic 5:1 ratio: stable couples maintain at least five positive interactions per negative conflict. | `social_psych` |
| 1094 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1997 | Judith Rich Harris: The Nurture Assumption Controversy | Judith Rich Harris, Psychological Review | Controversial thesis arguing peer groups and genetic inheritance dominate child development over parenting style. | `developmental_psych` |
| 1095 | 9. Neuroscience & Integration Era | 1997 | Martin Seligman: APA Presidential Address Launches Positive | Martin E.P. Seligman, San Francisco | Redirects psychology from curing mental illness pathology to nurturing human strengths, virtues, and flourishing. | `positive_psych` |
